# Agents · I · part 14

1,099 entries, issue-analyst to issue-tracker. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **issue-analyst**: Use during Kubernetes issue triage to classify what class of issue this actually is - bug, flake, regression, feature, docs, support or question - and to extract the exact claimed-vs-expected behavior, affected component and SIG, versions and environment, and what information the reporter left out. - **issue-analyst**: Reads a GitHub issue (body, comments, linked PRs and diff) and returns a readiness report for E2E testing: the acceptance criteria (taken verbatim from the issue, or derived from the details and the PR diff when it has none), the target environment, the mode (verify or reproduce), gaps, and the exact clarifying questions to ask. - **issue-splitter**: 將使用者提出的一堆問題／需求拆解成可獨立完成的小任務，若本地倉庫連結 GitHub 則用 gh 開 Issue，並把小任務派發給 niuma agent（約 Gemini 3.8 Flash 等級）執行；自己只做規劃、派工、驗收，不寫 code。只要使用者一次丟來多個問題、一個大而複合的需求、「幫我拆解／規劃／分派／開 issue」、或任務明顯大到單一輕量 agent 一次做不完，就務必使用此 skill，即使使用者沒有明說「拆解」。 ([YFHD-osu/dotfiles](https://github.com/YFHD-osu/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/dot_config/opencode/agents/issue-splitter.md))
- **issue-standard**: The single canonical standard for filing, labelling, and closing GitHub issues in `Tr3kkR/Yuzu`. Adopted by ADR-3001 (`docs/adr/3001-issue-lifecycle-guardrails.md`, as amended by A1). It binds humans and agents equally, by this text and PR review; the `gh` CLI path will additionally carry mechanical enforcement (a PreToolUse hook — ADR-3001 pillar 4, PR 3 of the stack; Claude sessions only — other ([DevNullLtd/Yuzu](https://github.com/DevNullLtd/Yuzu/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/issue-standard.md) · ★19)
- **issue-step-worker**: Implementation worker for a bounded issue step. Focus on the assigned step, use TDD when practical, and stop at the step boundary. ([SuiJapan/DeepARENA](https://github.com/SuiJapan/DeepARENA/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/issue-step-worker.md) · ★1)
- **issue-steward**: Maintains the issue queue (Belayline, STEWARDS.md §1): axis labels, intent, duplicates, routing, dependencies, so that a lot agent can take an issue without asking a question. Use on a new issue, every day, or before a wave of lots. ([francoisgn/belayline-agentic-harness](https://github.com/francoisgn/belayline-agentic-harness/blob/HEAD/plugins/harness/agents/issue-steward.md) · ★3)
- **issue-style**: How to write issue titles and bodies for this repo. ([emsqrd/f1fantasyapp](https://github.com/emsqrd/f1fantasyapp/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/issue-style.md))
- **issue-subagents-code-reviewer**: Code reviewer agent for issue-subagents skill. Feat classification only. One-shot read-only review of the diff against the spec; returns a structured verdict block with severity-and-confidence-tagged findings. The orchestrator decides whether to un-draft. ([Jonas-Ross/issue-team](https://github.com/Jonas-Ross/issue-team/blob/HEAD/agents/issue-subagents-code-reviewer.md))
- **issue-subagents-dev**: Dev agent for issue-subagents skill. One-shot per invocation — initial spawn implements the spec via TDD against acceptance tests/manual checks and pushes the branch; re-spawn fix-loop mode addresses review findings. Never opens, edits, or un-drafts the PR. ([Jonas-Ross/issue-team](https://github.com/Jonas-Ross/issue-team/blob/HEAD/agents/issue-subagents-dev.md))
- **issue-subagents-pm**: PM agent for issue-subagents skill. Feat-class only (PM-led roster). One-shot — reads the issue context, writes the spec, returns the path and a one-paragraph summary, exits. Does NOT author the PR body and does NOT brief other agents. ([Jonas-Ross/issue-team](https://github.com/Jonas-Ross/issue-team/blob/HEAD/agents/issue-subagents-pm.md))
- **issue-subagents-qa**: QA agent for issue-subagents skill. Two distinct one-shot modes selected by spawn prompt — test-author mode writes acceptance tests from the spec; review mode reviews the diff against the spec. Returns a structured result, never calls other agents. ([Jonas-Ross/issue-team](https://github.com/Jonas-Ross/issue-team/blob/HEAD/agents/issue-subagents-qa.md))
- **issue-summarizer**: Summarise a tracker issue. ([Goldziher/ai-rulez](https://github.com/Goldziher/ai-rulez/blob/HEAD/bench/judge/corpus/units/ben-s08-a/.claude/agents/issue-summarizer.md) · ★179)
- **issue-team-dev**: Dev agent for issue-team skill. Implements GitHub issue tasks using TDD against QA's acceptance tests. Routes ALL review signals through team-lead (the coordinator). Opens the draft PR with its own body, un-drafts only on team-lead's explicit authorization. ([Jonas-Ross/issue-team](https://github.com/Jonas-Ross/issue-team/blob/HEAD/agents/issue-team-dev.md))
- **issue-team-pm**: PM agent for issue-team skill. Feature-only. Writes the spec, waits for team-lead approval, briefs Dev and QA, then stays alive as the scope authority. Does NOT author the PR body and does NOT authorize un-drafting. ([Jonas-Ross/issue-team](https://github.com/Jonas-Ross/issue-team/blob/HEAD/agents/issue-team-pm.md))
- **issue-team-qa**: QA agent for issue-team skill. Writes acceptance tests from the spec before Dev implements. Classification-forked second responsibility — refactor/bugfix is PR review gate; feature is acceptance tests only. Reports ALL approval signals to team-lead, never to PM or Dev. ([Jonas-Ross/issue-team](https://github.com/Jonas-Ross/issue-team/blob/HEAD/agents/issue-team-qa.md))
